HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"quarantine_without_hft","docket_ready":true}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_001332/B001","root_001332/B002","root_001583/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_2ac06bbb9516a336f1dd","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"96:11:3\",\"root_001332\",[\"B001\",\"B002\"]],[\"96:11:5\",\"root_001583\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Doğru yol, yalnızca benimsenen bir düşünce değil, kişinin üzerinde durduğu ve koruduğu gerçek bir konumdur.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000531/B013","root_001332/B002","root_001583/B003","root_001583/B010"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_2adf7ec9f4f6328b25a8","text":"1. The inquiry tests whether the servant stands on a guided course. Evidence: `ر ء ي B013` alerting tell-me formula, `ك و ن B001` being/occurrence, `ه د ي B001` gentle guidance toward way/truth, `ه د ي B002` course/manner/intended direction. Structural cue: `على الهدى` gives guidance as a stable ground or course beneath the one being considered. Mechanism: the question reorients seeing from the forbidding actor to the possibility that the forbidden servant is already on a true path. Abductive move: the packet gives guidance as both truth and course; I infer that the ayah asks for moral/spatial reassessment. Reading change: it reads as “Have you considered if the blocked act is actually the right path?” 2. Guidance activates leading-front imagery against the forelock scene. Evidence: `ه د ي B003` leading front part/guide, later `ن ص ي B001` forelock/front hairline. Structural cue: guidance here precedes seizure of the front in 96:15-16. Mechanism: the guided front and the seized forelock become competing uses of the body's leading edge: one led by guidance, one dragged by falsehood. Abductive move: the packet supplies front/leading branches; I infer a body-direction mechanism. Reading change: `على الهدى` gains kinetic force: guidance is the true leader of the body before coercive seizure appears. 3. `كان` activates rank/place, making guidance a position one occupies. Evidence: `ك و ن B002` place/position/rank; `ه د ي B010` calm composed bearing. Structural cue: `إن كان على` combines being with “upon,” inviting a status-location reading. Mechanism: guidedness is a settled station, not merely a passing idea. Abductive move: the packet supplies place/rank branch; I infer that the servant's position is socially and spiritually elevated despite being obstructed. Reading change: the ayah becomes a status question: what if the one being blocked occupies the true place?","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001583/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_371a5110d29e4da20e92","text":"3. **Guidance as a course with a leading edge.** `ه د ي:B002` supplies course, manner, and intended direction, and `ه د ي:B003` supplies the leading front part or guide. Prayer precedes, command to protective caution follows, and 96:13 supplies the opposed movement `تولى`; 96:15–16 then focus the antagonist’s forelock. The guidance branches give the servant’s conduct a directed leading function, while the false “front” is later seized. I infer that embodied worship advances along the course it displays. `على الهدى` thus reads dynamically as being carried by a leading way.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001583/B002","root_001583/B003"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_3968e35a86eb4ba773bc","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"96:11:5\",\"root_001583\",[\"B002\",\"B003\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Doğru yol, kişiyi taşıyan yön ve önder ön olarak belirir; sonraki ilahî görüş, bu koşullu yargıyı daha üstün ve yanılmaz bir görmenin altına yerleştirir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000531/B002","root_000531/B013"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_7925dc9d9f6888306ef8","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"96:11:1\",\"root_000531\",[\"B002\",\"B013\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Koşullu 'gördün mü' sorusu, gözlemciyi engelleyen kişinin çerçevesinden çıkarıp engellenenin konumundan yeniden hüküm vermeye zorlar.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001583/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_87411f720ee8f42f0d2f","text":"96:13’s `تولى` supplied the exact opposite vector to `ه د ي:B002`, while 96:15–16 unexpectedly made the “leading front” branch `ه د ي:B003` concrete through the false forelock that is seized. 96:14 also corrected the provisional human `أرأيت` by placing the whole conditional under divine sight. Guidance consequently became both course and trustworthy leading front.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000531/B004"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_99a54f9021df8d67fe55","text":"4. **Humanly invited sight corrected by divine sight.** `ر ء ي:B004` supplies mutual visibility. The observer is repeatedly invited to see (96:9, 96:11, 96:13), but 96:14 states that Allah sees. The fixed question places human judgment inside a larger visual relation. I infer that the later clause corrects any uncertainty or partiality in the invited gaze. The ayah’s conditional examination is therefore provisional under a seeing that does not depend on the observer’s answer.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001332/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_9b78af75fb18a49ff1c6","text":"2. **Guidance as an occupied position.** `ك و ن:B001` supplies being or occurrence, while `ك و ن:B002` supplies place, position, or rank. The construction does not merely say that guidance exists; it says `كان على الهدى`, using `على` to place the subject upon it. The position branch makes guidance a stance that can be occupied and maintained. I infer stability from the spatial predication.
